Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   239\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   240\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   241\t        else:\n   242\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   243\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   244\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   245\t\n   246\t        if self.training:\n   247\t            #
